Masoyana qauna ta har abada naga ƙauna, A last littafina, naga masoya masu qaunar ci gabana, Allah ya qara buɗi da ƙauna, inji ta sambaɗo muku littattafai masu ɗan karen daɗi🤏🏻.

Ahankali ta tsallako kai tsaye kuma ta shige dogon layin dake gabanta, ta dubi uban tsayin  dogon layin duk da yawan al'ummar da yake da, amma gaba ɗaya anguwar shiru kakeji tamkar ba'a taɓa rayuwa acikin ta,haka take shiru duk da kuwa cewa anguwar marasa qarfi ce sosai.

Sannu Ahankali take ci gaba da taka qafafunta tamkar mai tausayin qasa,wanda Azahirance idan kaga yadda take takun nata zakayi tsammanin da gayya take takun.
Saidai sammm abun ba haka yake ba, haka halittar ta take idan tana tafiya kowa yana ganin tamkar tsabar kwaunane ce, Allah Allah take taga ta Isah gidansu, dan tasan yanxun duk inda hankalin ummanta yake to yana a kanta ne. duba da cewa yauɗin ta tashi a makare ne daga wurin aikin nata by 6.am aka magana da woni abu.

Babu wata doguwar tafiya ta Iso qafar gidan nasu,wanda ke rukunin gidajen marasa qarfi sosae acikin anguwar ƘOFAR DUMI dake cikin garin Bauchi.

Kanta tsaye ta nausa kanta cikin zauren gidan nasu, tana gyara ratayar jakarta dake maƙale a kafaɗarta, turus taja da tsaya sakamakon ganin ƙanwarta dije da tayi tsaye da wani matashin saurayin,,dake fuskantar ta wanda yanayin tsaiwarsu kaɗai ya isa ya shaida maka cewar fira suke yi, ɗauke kanta tayi tamkar bata gansuba, don wannan dabi'a ta dije kai bama itaba duk yaran gidan na bata haushi matuƙa.

Ta tsakiyar su ta raɓa ta huce, don  dole sai tabi tsakiyar nasun kana ta wuce cikin gidan nasu.
       Sauƙar muryar qanwar nata tayi tana faɗin "Haka dai aka iya kullum azira zumbulelen hijjabi, ace an tafi wata uwa aiki! Alhalin da inda ake zuwa ake fakewa da wani gurin aiki".
                
  Wani irin tsalle taji zuciyarta yayi tare da wani irin bugu, hakan yasa ta ɗago kanta ahankali tana sauqe idanunta, Akan dije babu yabo babu fallasa. shigewa cikin gidan  tayi ba tare data iya ɓunta mata koda Aa bane.
Sallama tayi cikin gidan kamar yadda ta saba duk da tasan babu mai amsa  mata, inhar ba ummanta bane a tsaƙar gidan ko Khadijah qanwarta wacce suke uwa ɗaya uba ɗaya.
In banda su babu mai amsa mata sallama, koda kuwa zata shekara dubu arba'in tana yi ɗin kuwa, aikuwa tamkar yadda ta tsammata ɗin, haka abin ya faru, don kuwa ko kallon arziƙi bata samu daga wurin matan BABAN NATA BA.

Bata fasa musu sannu da gida ba tamkar dai kullum ɗin, tsaki ladiyo da hajja suka ja atare, kanta kawai ta ɗan girgiza loqacin data tsinkayo muryoyinsu, suna sana'ar tasu wato munafincinta.

"Lallai idan da ranka kasha kallo yo wama ya sani ko an gama    gantalewa a saman kwalta, sannan  adawo gida simi simi cikin kwaton hujja i tamkar wata ta gari ana fakewa da wani uwa, wai shi aiki" 
    
Gatsinewa hajja tayi loqacin data gama jin maganganun ladiyon.

"Hmmmm ladiyo kenan nikam kaina mamaki kike bani wani bin, in banda abinki kina tunanin yarin ya mai jini ajika, maiji da tashen ƴan matanci kamar LAYLERH. Harma  kice yo ai babu tamtama tagama sanin takan harkar bariki."
     Wata uwar shewa ladiyo tayi tare da faɗin "shi yasa yinki hajja domin akwai fashimta"
 { Kunji fa Itan kenan bata da fashimtar.}

Tafawa sukayi tare da qara ɓarkewa da wata uwar shewar a tare.

     Da sallama bakinta ta shiga cikin ɗakin mahaifiyar nata, jin ba'a amsa ta bane yasa ta shiga cikin ɗakin kai tsaye.
Zaune ta tarar da ita bisa darduma  tana da Alqur'ani ahannunta tana karanta wa.
Zama tayi gefen mahaifiyar nata tare da cire toms ɗin ƙafarta.
       Daga ɗakin nasu suke jin irin wulaƙancin da sukaci gaba dayi, akan laylerh.

Daga har mahaifiyar ta babu wanda ya tankasu, daga laylerh har ummanta,  domin inda sabo yaci ace sun saba da irin wannan maganganun daga abokan zaman nata.

Fitowa tayi ta ɗauka buta ta ɗebo ruwan alwula tazo zata gitta su Hajja, ladiyo tace "Hmmmmm Allah wadaran naka ya lallace kuwa, dan wataran tabbas za'a tabka abin kunya a wurin nan".
Bata saurari zantukan da suke ta faɗi akan nata ba, duk da kuwa cewar Acen cikin ƙasan ranta maganganun da suke faɗi ɗin, akanta suya suke mata acikin zuciyarta. Amma haka ta share su tayi shigewarta ɗaki bayan ta gama alwular tan, darduma ta shimfiɗe tana tada sallah.
Bayan idar da sallan nata hannuwanta ta cira sama tana roqon Allah, ya kawo mata ɗauki cikin dukkan al'amurranta, tare da sauqaqa mata.
Ta daɗe tana kaima Allah kukanta kafin ta sallame.

    Jin sauƙar muryar mahaifiyartane kuma ya dawo da ita Cikin nutsuwarta, "Kiyi haƙuri MARYAMA! insha Allahu wata rana sai labari, kici gaba dayin haƙuri insha Allahu, Allah zai miki zaɓi na gari! Kullum cikin Adda'ar zaɓin MIJI na gari nake miki." 
      Cikin tausaya wa da ƙaryewar zuciya ummanta tayi maganar, don tana tsananin tausayin ƴar Tata sosai.

Cikin zallar hikima da tarin nutsuwarta tace,
   "Na gode da adda'oinki gareni ummana, na komai kada ki damu Allah yana sane da dukkan al'amuran. Kuma insha Allah shine zai kawo min mafita cikin halin tsananin da nake ciki".

 MARYAM YUSIF shine cikakken sunana na yanka, wanda ummana tamin laƙabi da LAYLERH.
Kasan cewar ashirin da biyar ga watan azumi ta haifeni, wanda Atsakankanin waɗannan kwana ake saka ran ganin, DAREN LAYLERTUL ƘADDR.
Wato daren Ashirin da biyar da kuma daren Ashirin da bakwai da daren Ashirin da tara! Inda Ni kuma mahaifiyata ta haifeni a daren Ashirin da biyar.
     Hakan ne yasa ta sanya min suna LAYLERH.
Mahaifina  yana da mata uku Hajja itace matarsa ta farko, kana sai mahaifiyata {Ummata} sai kuma ladiyo wacce yaran gidan suke ƙiranta da Inna ladiyo.

Mahaifiyata tasha wuya matuƙa awurin Hajja, dan babu irin izayar da bata gana mata ba. 
Haihuwar ta ɗaya Babana ya qaro wani auren, inda ya auro Inna ladiyo nan kuwa sabuwar duniya ta buɗe musu.
     Don sun ware mahaifiyata sunce ita JININ BAƘIN JINI ce. Tunda ƴaƴa  mata take haifa, yayin da su suka kasance ko waccensu tana da ƴaƴa maza.

Hajja haihuwar ta ta farko Namiji ne wato yaya jamilu sai INDO sai kuma Habibu sai Isah sai AUTAR Dije.
Yayinda Inna ladiyo take da yara huɗu ɗanta na fari itama  Namiji ne lawal sai kuma sani sai Kabiru sai kuma fatsima.
     Inda mahaifiyata taka sance haihuwarta ta farko ta haifi mace wato Ni MARYAM sai  kuma Khadijah AUTARMU, don ita haihuwarta biyu ne sai haihuwar ta tsaya mata cakk.

Inda gaba ɗaya wannan gida namu yake cike da tarin ƙalubale acikin sa.
Dan kuwa gida ne na rashin tarbiya qarara inda ya kasance gidan iya ba gwaba baba ba tausa, wato dai irin gidan nanne da na zaman kai da kai.
   Mahaifinmu alhaji yusif  {amma fa tun irin alhazan nan masu zuwa maka a JAKI babanmu yake}.

Ba irin alhazan yanxun masu zuwa a jirgi ba, yana da wani irin hali wanda ya kasance babu ruwan shi da haƙƙin matansa damu ƴaƴan shi. Wanda ko ƴar makarantar Arabi baya da ƴaƴan sa bare kuma boko, amma yace bai hana ba duk wacce take da kuɗinta, To kuwa bai hanata ba tasa ƴaƴan ta.
Wannan dalili yasa mahaifiyarmu cin akwatin ganin ci gabanmu, tun ina ƴar shekara biyar ta sanyani makarantar allon anguwarmu, Da kuma boko.
Sam bai damu da woni fidda  haƙƙi ba,  amma idan har yana dashi yakan kawo ko ba yawa a girka, inda sau da dama saidai muji qamshi daga nesa dan ba rabonmu ciki. Wanda hakan yake matuƙar ƙona raina fiye da tunanin mai karatu!. Zafin abinda ake ma mahaifiyata da kuma mu biyun data haifa, yasa naci alwashi akan zan tsaya tsayin daka wajen nema mana ƴancin Rayuwa da kuma gata.
    Wanda yanxun haka ina aikin  cike takardu a woni campany na J M  Company. 

Wannan kenan.

Babban Beach ne  mai girman gaske  daya amsa sunan shi,  BEACH ne babba daya samu world  recognition wanda kabbbbbbb duniyar ta bashi World Best Beach A shakarar 2019. Duk da kasan cewar na ɗaya daga cikin ƴan africa ne African ma 9j {Nigeria}.


      Gaba ɗaya wurin wani irin blue ɗin haske ne ke ta faman kaida komo a wurin, gefe guda kuma wani  irin haɗaɗɗan Swimming pool ne, wanda gaba ɗaya ruwan cikinsa ya kasance blue sosai.
Wata irin sanyin qamshine keta faman tashi, irin mai ratsa jikin nan tare da sanya nutsuwa a tattare da ma,abocin wurin.

   Wani kyakkyawan  matashin saurayine!  zaune daga bakin  ƙaton Swimming pool ɗin, sanye yake dawani Brown  shirt da farin dogon wando na Jessy.
Ya zira ƙafafuwanshi cikin ruwan, Ahankali yake karkaɗa qafafunsa cikin ruwan. A duk loqacin da yake cikin ɓacin rai ko kuma bege da kuma kewar abu mafi soyuwa acikin Rayuwarsa, yakan zo wurin ne don yakan ɗebe mashi kaso mafi rinjaye, na daga cikin abinda ke damunsa.

Aƙalla yafi ƙarfin sama da 40 mints awurin yana jin sassaucin abinda ke son taso mashi, duk da yana qoqarin dannewa tsayin shekaru masu ɗan dama.
 Jin barci nason fin qarfin sane yasa shi tashi yana layi, irin na masu barcin nan hannuwansa riƙe da wani  haɗaɗɗen diary, gaba ɗaya hankalin mafi rinjaye daga wurin ne ya dawo kansa, cikin sauri bodyguards nashi suka mimmiƙe tsaye, tare da saurin nufar parking space na cikin Beach ɗin,, wata farar  haɗaɗɗiyar Bugatti ɗaya daga cikin yaran nashi ya buɗe mashi, cikin tafiyar sassarfa ya kutsa kansa cikin lafiyayyar motar. Babu ɓata loqaci suka ja motocin aguje, ajere a kuma tsare suke tafiya suuuuuu cikin tsakiyar titin na qasar EGYPT 🪐.

    Basu yi wota tafiya mai zurfi ba suka isa wata qayatacciyar anguwa, anguwace guda ta musamman inda a bakin ƙaton Get na anguwar  yake ɗauke da harrufan,  L.... IN MY LIFE MENTIONED 9j. Horn suka buga get man yayi saurin buɗe musu get ɗin, tare da solid na motorn, ko gama daidaita parking basuy ba.
      Cikin sauri ɗaya daga cikin guards  nashi ya fito ya buɗe mashi murfin motorn.
Qafafuwanshi ya fara zirowa kafin ya fito baki ɗayansa.
Eyephone nashi kawai ya iya ɗauka tare da yin gaba abunshi.
 
    Babban parlourn su ya nufa cikin sauri² gudu² yake tunkarar parlourn gidan, saidai me yana sanyo kansa yaja yayi turus, ganin  gaba ɗaya ploorn ahar mutse tamkar kullum, da kwalayen picanto, dominos pizza for lunch ya fara cin karo.
Hakan ya bashi tabbacin tun lunch na rana ɗaya fita ya barine bata gyara ba.
Ɗan ƙaramin tsoki yaja ganin gaba ɗaya komi da komi na parlourn babu abinda yake a kimtse, 

Numfasawa yayi kaɗan, aransa yana faɗin shikam, ya rasa wacce iriyar MATA ce FADEELAH! Gaba ɗaya rayuwanta bata san daidai d rashin daidai ba. Deda trow pillows idan ya faɗo daga saman sopa bata iya ɗaukewa,  every where is unkept and scatterd ko kaɗan babu kimtsi acikin rayuwarta.

      Hakan yasa cikin wani irin zafin zuciyar daya daɗe, yana dannewa ya nufi cikin bedroom nata, wanda tsabar azabar zafin rai yasa har step bibbiyu yake takawa, afusace ya turo ƙofar ɗakin tare da ware idanunsa. Wanda sukafi kama dana masu jin barci, dan koda yaushe every time suna alumshe ne. Ja yayi ya tsaya ganin ya kwance cikin wasu matsiyatan kayan barci, wanda dasu kara babu kuma yana da tabbacin haka tayi ta yawonta dasu a cikin gidan, Sam-sam fadeelah batasan rayuwa ba shikam dade da rana ɗaya bata taɓa fito ta faɗi batare da sunyi rikici ba.

"FADEELAH! FADEELAH!! FADEELAH!!!" 

Ya shiga ƙiran sunanta tare da jijjaga ta, cikin tsabar rashin tarbiya ta shiga faɗin.

"JALAAL AREEF what happened? Meye damuwarka dani ne kammm! Kake min wannan uban qiran, yau kuma dame kazo? Dan nasan akullum Ni bana rasa laifi awurin ka! Duk kuwa da cewa ina iyaka bakin qoqarina, wajen ganin na faranta maka".

    Kasa cewa uffan yayi saima zuba mata lumsassun idanunsa da yayi, tare da sarƙe hannuwansa cikin ƙirjinsa,  yama rasa bakin magana. 

       Ɗan numfasawa yayi tare da tattara dukkan kalmomin da yakeson amfani dasu, wajen ganin ya fashimtar da fadeelah irin girman laifukanta gare shi.

  Hakan yasa shi fara magana cikin hosky voice nashi yace.

    " Mene kika ɗauki aure ne kammm? Dasam bakisan kima da kuma darajar shiba,  kullum ke burin ki kiyi ta chart da ƙawaye, amma Sam sam bakisan yadda ake kula da miji ba, but kawai abinda kika sani shine kiyi sleeping kiyi chatting!, bana ce tunda aka auroki kin taɓa koda kwatankwacin shiga kitchen ne, da sunan girki   dade pant and Bra naki  masu aiki ke wanke miki.
   Waikam ke ko kunya ma baki jine don Allah?.
        Na gaji da dukka halayenki marasa ƙyau FADEELAH, dan haka ya zamar miki dole ki tsaya ki fuskanci rayuwa yadda ya kamata, idan har kinason cigaba da zaman aure dani!".

  Cikin zallar iya tsageranci da sakalci ta mashi, kallon sama da qasa tare da faɗin.

 "Ka gama?"

Ko ikon magana bata samu ya mata ba hakan yasa ta qara fusata.

 Zaune ta miƙe tana tattaro uban gashin dokin dake kitse akan, ta fara magana cikin rashin tarbiya da rashin girmama na gaba dakai.

 "Kai ko kunya bakaji ba don Allah da kace na shiga kitchen?. Kana a matsayin THE BILLIONAIRE of African kai harda ma sauran qasashen waje, amma kake maganar only you ni MATARKA FADEELAH JABBER SHETTIMA na shiga kitchen?, Sannan nayi worshing, nayi  Garbage,  washed and washed?.
       Dama baiwa aka kawo maka kenan, to me amfanin DUKIYAR da ALLAH ya baka Keenan? Idan har zan shiga kitchen kana nayi duk sauran aikin gidanka. Kaga Keenan babu amfanin DUKIYAR da muke da, idan har nice ƴar aikinka JALAAL AREEF THE BILLIONAIRE!"

Numfashi yake sauƙewa  domin ganin abubuwa da yawo na neman suyi,  crosing mind nashi  marasa daɗi. Musamman abubuwan dake faruwa a zaman auren shi shida FADEELAH , yasan dole ne yayi zaman aure da fadeelah tunda zaɓin mahaifiyar sace. Ƴar ƙanwar tace Hajiya Rabi'atou Aliyu.

Idanunsa ya lumshe tare da buɗesu akanta, yarrr tsikar jikinta suka zuba ganin irin kallon da yake binta dashi, baiyi aune ba yaji shi jikinta ta janyo shi saman gadon tare da haɗe shi da jikinta.
     Kunnensa ta kama ta shiga raɗa mishi "haba my only you, ka tsaya kayi tunani da ƙyau muyi rayuwarmu cikin hutu dajin daɗi! Allah ne ya hutar damu dan haka kaga dole ne mu huta"
Hugging nashi tayi tare da faɗin 
"good night baby  dreams, kayi barci mai daɗi kaga Ni ka tadani da  hayaniyar kan, gashi kaga ina off bare ka rage Zafi"
  Ta faɗi hakan tana juya mashi baya.
{Niko AMMEY LAYLERH nace mene rage Zafi kuma fan's🥰}
  Haushi baƙin ciki takaici sune suka taru suka tokare maƙoshin JALAAL , wato ma mayen mata ta ɗauke shi kenan ko me, dan kawai tana matar shi shine take da zarrar faɗa masa duk kalaman da suka zo cikin bakinta. Shikenan zaiyi maganin ta.

Nan ya tashi tare da zare tshrt ɗin jikinsa tare da dogon wandon, comfy white towel ya ɗaura tare da shigewa cikin bathroom...
Shower kawai ya sakar ma kansa, kafin daga bisani ya tara ruwa masu ɗumin gaske acikin jacuzzyn, duba da yanayin garin wanka yayo mai rai da lafiya tare da shafe jikinsa da tularenv dolcy & gabbana  kayan barci mai santsi ya zura, tare da ficewa a ɗakin nata baki ɗaya, ɗakinsa ya nufa yayi kwanciyar shi hankali kwance, ya daɗe yana tunanin rayuwar aurensu da fadeelah  for the past 3 years, amma ace samm mutum bai fuskanci gobensa ba bai kuma san miye rayuwa ba. Ita dai kawai taci mai ƙyau tasha mai ƙyau ta tonƙoshe ƙafafu tayi chatting ta sani, in banda hakan babu abinda ta sani, dade da school nata ta bari, tace dama sabo da kuɗi ake karatu gashi ita kuma tana auren THE BILLIONAIRE of African. To karatun mene zatayi kuma turanci dai ta iya kana babu abinda mai master's zasu gaya mata.

A haka da wannan tunane tunanen har barci ɓarawo yazo yayi awon gaba dashi, ba tare daya ankare ba.....cen ɓangaren oga fadeelah kuwa tana jin fitar shi ta tashi zaune daram tana jan tsuka, wayanta ta ɗauko daga qarqashin fillow,  Deta nata ta kunna tana faɗin.
     "Shi mutum tamkar woni doki samm ace baya gajiya da abu ɗaya"

WhatsApp nata ta shiga taci gaba da chatting ɗinta da ƙawayenta ƴan duniya, sai wajajen ɗaya da rabi na dare tayi barci hakan ma ba dan taso ba.
********
        Cikin tafiyarsan na sauri²gudu² yake taka mata kalan benen babban parlourn nashi wanda ya gaji da haɗuwa,  gaba ɗaya malale yake  da wani irin ƙayataccen grass carpet  mai tsananin azabar ƙyau da ƙayatuwa. 
    Cikin zallar nutsuwar da Allah (S .W. T) yamar yaci gaba da saƙƙowa yana kuma tunkaro ƙayataccen parlourn gidan baki ɗaya.
Kai tsaye bayan gama sauƙowarsa palon, ƙofar ɗakin ta ya kalla, har yayi kamar  ya fice sai kuma wani tunani ya faɗo masa, hakan yasa shi nufar ƙofar ɗakin nata tare dayin Knoking, jin shiru ba'azo anbude mishi bane yasa shi tura ƙofar, dan yasan idan zai shekara yana Knoking ba zata taɓa buɗe mashi ba.
       Ƙafafuwan sa ya sanya cikin parlourn nata, turus yaja ya tsaya ganin tun kayan ƙazantar ta na jiya na Nan a parlourn, babu abinda aka gyara hakan yashi ajje ƙaramar jikar dake riƙe a hannunsa tare da zare farar rigarsa, gaba ɗaya saida ya tabbatar ya gama gyara parlourn tass, babu inda ya saura kana ya nufi bedroom nata.

    A hankali yake saƙƙowa daga matakalen benen cikin jirgin fadeelah biye dashi a bayan sa, wai wani hura hanci sama take taci uban wani spec daya kusan cinye rabin fuskarta

      Wasu uban  jibga jigban motocine da aƙalla zasu iya kai guda goma sha biyar, suka Parker  cikin ƙanƙanin loƙaci airport ɗin ya kuma kacamewa da matakan tsaro, na JALAAL da kuma mahaifinsa,, wanda dan danan sukawa JALAAL ƙawanya suka sanyashi a tsakiyar su tare da manyan bindugu a hannun su.

Wato anguwar GUZAPE ASOKORO DISTRICT ABUJA.
Gaban wani ƙaton Get wanda aka ƙawatashi sosae sojojine birjik  agaban ƙaton Get ɗin, riƙe da manyan bindugu kallon farko zaka ɗauka SOLDIER'S HOUSE ne, amma duba da yadda sojojin ke tsaye kuma ga mutane sai faman kaida komo suke, agidan shine kaɗai zai tabbatar maka cewa ba SOLDIER'S HOUSE bane.
ESTATE ne guda na duka familynsu JALAAL, babban gidane sosae mai ɗauke da rassa da yawa, cikin ƙanƙanin loƙaci aka wangale musu ƙaton Get ɗin. Motocin suka shigo abin mamaki wani dogon titine awurin abin sha'awa kallon farko zaka iya cewa wata anguwace, domin titine mai tsawo sunyi.
    Sunyi tafiya mai nisa kafin suka Isa wurin wani Get shima babbane sosea sojoji zagaye dashi.
Direct parking space waɗannan gungun motocin suka nufa,wanda aka qawata wurin da shuke ² da wasu decoration masu ɗaukar hankali, kai tsaye suka Parker motocin nasu.
        Saurin fitowa bodyguards nashi sukayi , suka buɗe motar da tafi ko wacce mota haɗuwa da ƙyau awurin, Cikin Isa yasanwo ƙyawawan daren sawayensa waje, kafin ya fito baki ɗayansa. Da sauri bodyguards nashi suka zagaye shi harda ma police wanda suke acikin gidan.

    "My MODDIBO Bismillah"

"Barta taje gurin uwartata don  uban daya haifi UBANTA!, nifa mamaki kike bani AISHA esheeeee dan kiji ma da ƙyau ba gulmarki  nayi ba uhmmmmm"
     Ta faɗi maganar tana gyara ɗaurin zanin ta dake ƙoƙarin since mata
 Tamkar daga sama haka sukaji sauqar balbalin bala'in Hajjiya Mama, gaba ɗayansu suka juyo suna kallon ta da mamaki, don babu wanda yasan da xuwanta cikinsu.

"Ba ita shegiya baaa ƴar yau, yoni zata gayawa yau nida naga jiya naga gobe, haka za inasa ran ganin jibi ma daram, nafa gama fashimta ba tun yauma A'ISHA tsoron duk mutanen gidan nan kike! Yo inba don nima kinkai ma labarine, haka ranar waccen shigiyar safarar mai kama da tsinken bawa tazo ta gama qare miki tacewarta, amma uffan kince mata ne?, gaba ɗaya ayi mutum sai shegen sanyin tsiya"

sai kuma ta shiga kuka tare da face majina tana faɗin
 "ALLAH Sarki AREEF nasan da kana raye da babu mai abinda ko wacce mace ta isa koda yiwa A'isha kallon wulaƙancin neeee, ALLAH dai yaji qanka yasa ka huta! Wannan yaro"

"Don girman Allah Mama ki rage sanya damuwata akanki! Inajin babu daɗi sosae, domin duk abinda ake min wallahi bana jin zafinsa, kamar yadda kike sanya damuwata ta zama taki Mama"
   JALAAL dake tsaye ne ya qara so yana zama kusa ta kakar tashi, tare shima da fara bata bakin kamar yadda yaga UMMIEN sa nayi.

         "Wallahi JALALU  sai nasa kayi maganin shegiyar matar nan taka ita da banzar uwarta, da sammm bada hali na gari dan nikam ba'a NoNo na tashayo baƙin halinta ba, kuma nasan maganinta daga ita har sauran matan BABAN naka! Da sannu duk sainayi maganin su"

"JALALU Ni niiiiiiiii uwar UBAN ka kake gayawa magana?, yo dama ai nasan ba sona kake ba, inbanda abinka ba uwarka nake shigarwa faɗan ba!      "Sai uwarka nace sai uwarka MODDIBO, Hajiya Maman kake faɗawa maganar daka ga dama? Ashe dama baka da mutunci MODDIBO, ka wuce kabe muta ne wuri ko saina ɓara ranka!"

     JALAAL

Hankali kwance cikin nutsuwarsa na mutumin daya gama sanin kansa,  yake tunkarar ƙaton ploorn nashi.
 "ya rabbi" na faɗa loqacin da hancina ya gama shaqo mini dauwamammen qamshim sa wanda ya riga da ya gama kama ɗakin, parlour ne haɗaɗɗe domin  iya haɗuwa kam ya haɗu don alqalamina bazai taɓa iya rubuta muku tsaruwar ploorn ba, idan kuma nace zan tsaya rubuta muku ya tsaruwar ploorn yake, to ashe kammm zamuy kwanan zaune ne kawai abinda nasani, kowannen ku yayi Emeg acikin RAI da kuma RUHINSA.

Paulour mae girman gaske wanda yake 
   ɗauke da sassa guda uku,  wanda ko wanne ɓangaren da irin qawatuwar da yake da kana idan mutum yana tafiya, yana kallon kansa da kansa ne ta cikin glass mirror ɗin ɗakin, wanda shi kuma na ciki kai tsaye yake sanin wanda yake tafen, shi kuma mai tunkarar ɗakin iya kansa da kansa  kawai yake kallo.
      
     Directly qofar tsakiyar parlournan uku ya nufa bi ma'ana qofar tsakiya kenan, ta yadda sauran sassan suka sanyashi tsaƙiyarsu kenan.

***Tunda yake tunkarar qofar  nake iya qare mashi kallo, ya shimfiɗa wani irin ni'imantaccen murmushi saman kyakkyawar fuskarshi, idanunsa tsaye akan qofar bangaren hannun damansa, wanda akawa qofar wani irin decoration na musamman.
Tun kamin yakai ga qofar shiga ploorn qofar glasses mirrorn ta fara wani irin rubuta da  red and blue colors, inda take rubuta  ~welcome to JALAAL AREEF place~  da wani irin style gwanin burgewa ga ba'acin kallon wurin.
    Saita ƙwayar Idanunsa yayi da ta wani ɗan sisirin abu inda aka zagaye abin in a style akayi abun, kana cikin sillent voice nashi yace.

    "you on accepted back JALAAL AREEF"
Zuuuuu qofar tayi sama baki ɗayanta, subhanallah shine kawai abinda baƙina ya iya furta wa, domin wani irin mahaukacin ploor ne wanda yaci uwar ma farkon komi da komai, gaba ɗaya ploorn an ƙawatashi da wasu Italian fashion rug masu masifar haɗuwa da burgewa, ɓangare gudan bangon ɗakin gaba ɗaya anshafe da plasma wanda idan har ba sani mutum yayi ba, bazai taɓa cewa plasma bace loqaci guda.
 Daga ɗayan gefen   kuma wani irin  haɗaɗɗan   dining area ne  inda aka masa adon ƙoyaye masu ɗan girma suka sakko qasa kaɗan da dining ɗin,  ɗaya ɓangaren kuwa wani irin azababben beni ne wanda yayi wani irin lankwasa, wanda kai tsaye zai sada mutum da bedroom ɗin hasalin ploorn kenan, gefe kaɗan da wurin kuma an ajje wani ƙaton dum lightning ne shima domin bada haske kaɗan,  wanda idan ma'abocin wurin baison haske yakan sanja hasken ya zuwa dum light ɗin kamar dai da night kenan.
wanda mutun A kallon farko yakan  iya bama wannan ploorn aljannar duniya, domin iya tsaruwa ploorn ya tsaru kuwa.
     Kai tsaye JALAAL yake taka mata kalar benen cikin sassarfa, don ya gama ƙosawa ya ganshi cikin bedroom nashi, babban burin sa a yanxun bai  fice ace yaji shi cikin ruwa mai ɗumi ba kana ya ganshi yana barci domin hutawa ba.
       
        Ya ilahi kai wannan gida gaba ɗayansa da azabar ƙyau yake baki ɗayansa, yana sanya kansa cikin hasalin bedroom nashi kuwa  da wani haɗɗaɗɗe lafiyayyan gado naci qaro, gadone iyaka gado amma irin ɗan dadai misalin nan, ba irin wargajejen Nan  na six by six bane daga saman gadon kuwa wata haɗaɗɗiyar benc Centre ce mai lokoki wanda samanta akayi wasu  qananun sink  guda biyu a wurin, sai kuma tsakiyarsu akayi adon wata madaidaiciyar fulawa,  ɗaya gefen bedside drawer ɗin kuma wani ƙaton Electric kindle ne, mai bama ɗakin ɗan qaramin haske da night shima irin wanda yake sassanja colors Innan.
     
          Cikin muryarsa wacce ta zama slocoly loqaci guda ya furta "ALHAMDULILLAH" yana jin wata iriyar nutsuwa na sauƙar masa ta Musamman, tare da sanya hannunsa yana shafa lallausan sajen dake kwance akan kyakkyawar fuskarshi.
       Sau cikin qafarsa ya shiga zarewa tare da fara  ɓalle aninayen maɓallan gaban rigarsa, tare da zare dogon wandon getner ɗin shima ya rage daga shi sai boxes da farar best ɗinsa, direct toilet yace ruwa masu ɗumin gaske ya haɗama kansa tare da zuba tularen wanka acikin. Iumsassun idanunsa da akoda yaushe suke a lumshe ya kuma lumshe wa, jinsa cikin ruwan  ɗumin gaba ɗaya ɗumi da kuma ƙamshim tularen, suka taru suka kuma sanya mashi wata iriyar nutsuwa, Ahankali ya buɗe idanun nashi kuma jin wani babban al'amari na shirin sanyo masa kai.
        "Ya Allah" ya faɗi hakan yana wani irin lumshe idanun tare da buɗesu ahankali,  Ahankali abinda yake taso masan ya fara qoqarin cin galabarsa hakan yasa shi fara furta wasu adda'oi daga cenn qasar zuciyarsa, "Ya ilahi" ya kuma furta wa karo na ba adadi jin wani irin azababben ciwon mara, da sauri ya dafe saitin marar nashi tare da datse lips ɗinsa da qarfin masifa, innalillahi ya shiga furta wa cikin muryar galabaituwa  da azabtuwa.

Cikin ƙanqanin loqaci gumi ya gama rufe shi shirgat, a daddafe ya qarisa yin Wonkan nashi tare da zarar towel ruwan madara yana ɗaura shi saman waist nashi,  ahankali ya mirza handle ɗin toilet ɗin tare da ficewa daga cikin toilet ɗin baki ɗaya,  kwantaccen gashin kansa sai ɗigar ruwa yake ko zaman tsane shi bai tsaya Yiba, gaban wani wuri ya wanda da dukkan halamu ya gama bayyana cewar wannan wurin wurine na musamman, ahankali ya shiga dandana wasu mabuɗan  sirri danda nan  abun ya buɗe nima kaina bansan abinda yayi, kawai dai naga ya zira wani abune mai kamar littafi.

Da sauri ya tashi a wurin yana faɗawa saman haɗaɗɗen austra bed nashi, juyi kawai yake faman yi haka har barcin wahala yazo yayi awon gaba dashi.

        **************
FADEELAH
*************

    A guje take jan motorn tamkar zata tashi sama saboda azabar fushin da take a ciki, gudu take bana hankaliba ikon Allah ne kaɗai ya kawota gida lafiya, mahaifiyarta suna Zazzau ne tare da yaranta biyu sai mai gidan Alhaji JABBER SHETTIMA mahaifi ga fadeelah kenan, kawai sai ganinta sukayi ta shigo a guje tare da zubewa jikin mahaifiyarta.
   Gaba ɗaya hankulan su idan yayi dubu toya gama tashi, hakan yasa cikin kiɗima suka shiga tambayarka lafiya, cikin son qara ingiza mahaifiyarta ga tsanar UMMIE ta fashe da kuka tare da cewa.
         "Mum wallahi matar Nan bazata taɓa ganin daidai ba a rayuwarta! bata sona ta tsaneni! Bansan mina mata ba gaba ɗaya ta shiga tsakanin mutane, ta rabaki da mahaifiyarmu!¡! Sannan ta haɗa kowa da Hajj Mama.
     Daga zuwanmu ta koroni wai wai wai na bar mata ɗanta ya huta  haka nan nima naje wajen tawa UWAR tunda ina da ita nima! Sannan inajin wannan makirar tsohuwar tana ta zaginmu nidake wai fa har cewa tayi Allah saiya saka mata haihuwarki da tayi, ita kum"
        Tsittt tayi sakamakon jin an kai ma bakinta wani irin shegen bugu, A fusace mahaifinta Alhaji JABBER SHETTIMA yace , "Fadeelah FADEELAH Fadeelah wai samm mai yasa ahar  kullum kike son kawoma mahaifiyar ki qarar surukuwarki!,, bayan na tabbata darajarta da kika cine harya sanya ABDUL-JALAAL Yarda da AURENki, amma ke burinki bai wuce ace kin kawo saɓañ"
   "Dakata Alhaji JABBERU  kafa daɗe kana shiga cikin hurumin daba baka ba! I na ruwan ka da family na ina Ruwan ka da rayuwar cikin gidanmu Meye naka Acikin?" 
    Mum ce ta katse shi ta hanyar dakatar dashi daga maganar tashi, wanda bata barima ya qarasa zancen nashi ba

    Cikin ɓacin rai ya miƙe tsaye tare da faɗin "kin kyautata ma kanki kenan yanxun yaranki ace sune masu son haɗaki da mahaifiyarki da matar babban yayanki, wanda yanxun take matsayin surukuwarki, kin haɗa da wasu shashashun Banza wanda suke shirin ganin sun  tarwatsa miki rayuwarki, kota halin yayane! Amma dake ke banza ce baki ganewa, kodan irinku dama Allah baya taɓa baku rabon shiriya!, saidai ya nuna muku ishirarsa da kuma ikonsa akanku, Anan ne zaku gane cewar Allah ɗaya yake , aloƙacin da baku da damar tube masa saidai kuji kunyar duniya, cen kuma kuje ku tarar da hisabinsa mafi tsanani!"
    Allah ya shirye duk wani mutum ɓata gari maison ganin, shi saiya ga bayan waninsa ameern ya rabbil alamin.

Ahankali yaɗan ware lumsassun Idanunsa kaɗan tare da laluben wayar nashi, cikin barcin da bai gama sakin saba cenñnn qarshen gado ya jiyota, hakan yasa ya janyo ya anutse yana amsa qiran kai tsaye ba tare da yasan waye mai qiran ba.
A ƙalla yakusa ɗaukan 20 minute da wayar a kunnensa, sai kuma kawai ya rumtse idanunsa da qarfin masifa, kafin jikinsa ya ɗauki wani irin karkarwa tamkar mai rawar ɗari, tuni idanunsa ya sanja launi daga farare xuwa jawur, gaba ɗaya jijiyoyin kansa kuwa sun tashi sunyi raɗa².
      Ba tare danaji  mene aka faɗamar daga cikin wayar ba, amma dai da alamun koma mene aka gaya masa mai girma ne.

Wani irin zazzafan numfashi ya fesar  sabo da  jin kalaman na barƙa dukkan wata  lakar dake jikinsa, nan yaji wani irin abu  na sauƙa tun daga ƙwaƙwalwarsa har xuwa tafin qafafunsa, inda gaba ɗaya yaji jikin sa yayi wani irin sanyin da tunda yake bai taɓa jin koda kwatankwacin hakan ba. Fesar da numfashi yayi  mai zafi tare da zare wayar a kunnensa yana jifa da ita baki ɗayan, kansa ya tallafo ta hanyar dafesu da dukka hannuwansa biyun  wani irin ja idanunsa sukayi ga kuma wani ruwa ruwa da suka tara suna ƙallin wahayen da suka taran masa. Shi kansa bazaice ga yadda yake ji acikin RAI da kuma RUHINSA saba, kawai abinda ya sani zuciyarsa tayi masa wani irin nauyin da yake tunanin ko haɗiyar yawu bazai taɓa haɗiyewa ba, cike da matsanancin tashin hankali marar misaltawa kana cikin galabaituwa ya fara jero dukkan adda'oin da suka mashi.

  "Ya ilahi ya mujibud da'awati, ya hayyu ya qayyum bi rahma tika astaghisu, la haula wala quwwata illa billah!"

 Haka yayi ta duk wata Adda'ar ta tazo bakinsa, faɗar irin tashin hankalin da yake ciki ma faɗa loƙacine, amma kai tsaye bazaka taɓa fuskantar halin da yake aciki ba, kasancewarsa mutum mai taurin zuciya yasa sammm farat ɗaya ba zaka taɓa fuskantar yanayin da yake aciki ba.
      Sai kuma ya shiga sakin ajiyar zuciya akai akai alamun zuciyarsa ta saussauta daga irin zafin da take yimar, ahankali ya shafa sajen dake kwance a kuncinsa, wanda yake fidda wani irin shining ɗin kyau da iyakar gyaran da yake sha.

   ←←←→→→
 Cikin matsanancin kuka take faɗin.
    "Don Allah! don darajar  Annabi Muhammad Sallallahu alaihi Wasallama baffa kayi haƙuri kada  ka auramin  Iliya!.
    Na yarda ka auramin koma waye amma banda Iliya, baffa Iliya ɗan shaye shaye ne manemin mata ne nasan kasan hakan, baffa koda makaho ko kuturu ka auramin Wallahi na yarda zan rayu dashi amma don darajar fiyayyen halitta banda Iliya!, baffa kasan waye Iliya kuwa? mutumin daya kasance, You are a drunkard, you are looking for women and an infidel who does not pray, na haɗaka da GIRMAN UBANGIJIIN sammai bakwai kayi haƙuri baffa!

 Ta karashe maganar 
   Cikin matsanancin kuka, laylerh take roƙôn mahaifin nata, shi kuwa cikin ainihin gaskiyarsa yace mata.
  
           "Saidai kiyi haƙuri MARYAMU aure nekam babu fashi insha Allah, domin na gaji da ganin ki haka Nan na gaji idan na barkin waye zaki aura? Yaushe kuma zakiyi AUREN? To Ni gaskiya na gaji da ciyar da balagaggiya kamarki  ke wai ko haushin kanki baki jine kammmm!, ki duba fa ki gani gaba ɗaya ƙannenki duk sunyi aure daga masu ƴaƴa biyu sai masu ƴaƴa uku don haka kuwa dole ne na aura miki Iliya!'
     Ya qare maganar yana shigewa cikin ɗakinsa .

Inna ladiyo da hajja kuwa   farin cikin duniya ya gaba isarsu, tabbas boka yayi gaskiya domin dama yace musu zasuga irin MIJIN da MARYAM ɗin zata aura, kana yace musu koda wasa babu wani mahalukin daya isa yazo wurinta da sunan zance. 
{Kunji shegu sun manta cewar  Allah shine mai kowa mai komai}
   
A tare suka tafa tare da sakin shewa, sai kuma suka ɗauki guda irin ta ƴan duniyar nan tare da fara yada wasu baƙaƙen maganganu marasa daɗinji.
Umma kuwa dake zaune a cikin ɗakinta murmushi mai ciwo ta saki, tare da ci gaba da karatun Alqur'ani mai girma da take karantawa. Kamar kazar da kwai ya fashewa haka laylerh ta shigo ɗakin tare da zama kusa da umman, tana zamewa ta ɗora kanta bisa qafafun umman nata, cikin son ta kwantarma da yarinyarta hankali ta shiga lallashinta, tare da ce mata taci gaba da adda'a insha Allah tanaji acikin jikinta Allah zai musanya mata da mafi Alkhairinsa, haka dai tayi ta  mata nasihohi masu ratsa zuciya a qarshene kuma ta ɗago idanun laylan tace mata.

*"Kiyi haƙuri Maryam! ƙarshen haƙuri nasarace, ƙarshen tsanani darajace, ƙarshen wahala ɗaukakace, ƙarshen  jarabawa kuma Alkhairi ne, don haka ki kasance cikin yaƙini da kuma godiya ga Allah"*


"Baffa! baffalo!! Baffalota!!!"

Lawandine ya shiga rarakawa mahaifin nashi qira tamkar maqogwaran sa zai fito, cikin buɗaɗɗiyar muryarsa irin ta mashaya yaci gaba da faɗin.

"Wai kana ina neeeeee  nasan kana jina, idan an isa ayi wannan auren shege nake laylan za'a aurawa Iliya!, Iliyan da kowa ya sani mugun ɓarawone! Ɗan daba!! Wanda kabbb garin nan babu wanda baisan shiba, yaron da kusan kullum yana hanyar birsin, ƴan daba fa yake ma aiki to wallahi yau dai nima sainawa Maryam ɗin da mahaifiyarta Rana "

  Sai kuma ya shiga kwalama mahaifiyarsa qira cikin gabjejiyar muryarsa data gama buɗewa.

    "Inna! Inna!! LADIYOOO! Danma kin samu ana ce miki Innan to ladiyo fita kina INAAAAAA, nasan wannan ba aikin kowa bane daya wuce ke da wannan shegiyar hajjar"

  Inna ladiyo kuwa da tunda taji shigowar ɗan nata ta shige qarqashinsu gadon ko ƙwaƙƙwaran motsi bata kuma yiba, saida taji yana zabba mata wannan uban qiran ne kuma bata san sanda ta saki fitsarin azabar tsora ba.
 Hajja kuwa da tayi wani irin tambulan ta shige cikin macaccen sif ɗinta, ɗaya riga ya gama macewa, cikin tsananin tsoro ta shiga zazzara ido tamkar wacce tayi ma sarki sata.

Shi kuwa lauwali yaci gaba da faɗin.
  "YARINYAR da kakaf gidan daga ita sai mahaifiyarta ne kaɗai ke qaunata, amma ina raye ace za'a aura mata Iliya ɗan shola shili kafuri kuma !"

    Cikin mazarin jiki da matsananci tsoron ɗan nashi baffa dake ɓoye a bayan ƙyauren ɗakin sane yasa shi fara magana cikin tsoran ɗan nashi.
 "Wannan anyi ɗan shegiyar yaro, samm ganinka babu alkhairi acikin sa"
shifa har mantawa yake da yana da wani ɗa lauwali.
Umma dake zaune har yanzu ce ta miƙe tana fita a ɗakin don xuwa wurin lauwalin da kafa gidan maganar tane kaɗai yake ji, acikin sassanyar muryanta wanda ya qara sanyi fiye da da ta nufo shi tare da faɗin.
 "Haba lawan ayi haquri haka nan mana kowanne bawa yana da nashi irin kaɗɗarar, ita kuma nata a haka yazo mata, idan har wannan aure shine mafi Alkhairinta to Ubangijii ya tabbatar mana shi da Alkhairi!"
   Ganin ita ma  umman yau  tanason ta ɓata masa raine yasa yace mata.
"Taya auren Iliya zai zama Alkhairi umma?, bayan kinsan irin abubuwan da yake aikatawa na rantse idan da wanda ya isa ya ɗaura auren Nan shege nake, idan kuwa anyi to na rantse niba ƊAN HALAK bane SHEGE ne"

   Fuuuuuuuuuuuu ya fice a gidan yana fita kuwa yaransa suka rufa mishi baya.
************
       Zaune yake gaban  ummiensa  cikin tattara dukkan nutsuwarsa zuwa ga mahaifiyar nashi yace.
"Ummie  wannan shine kaɗai alfarmar da zakimin!, kana kamar yadda na faɗa miki haka nakeson abun ya kasance, kuma Ni da kaina zance wurin baba hashimun na sanar dashi!"
  Cikin wani irin yanayi ummien ta kalla tilon yaron nata, kana tace.
  "ABDULJALAAL kana ganin babu wata matsala kuwa, idan har muka ɓullo ta wannan al'amarin!?"
Ta qare zancen tana jin tamkar hakan baiyi mata ba, cikin tabbatar wa yace mata.
"Idan harba ta wannan hanyar ba to kuwa bazan taɓa samun cikin muradina ummienaaaa" 
    Yaja sunan nata, cikin yarda tace na amince maka  ABDULJALAAL"
Cike da tsananin farin ciki marar misaltawa ya rubgume mahaifiyar nashi tare da cewa.
   "Na gode ummiena Ubangijii ya biya ki da gidan aljannatul Firdausi!"

***********
   BAUCHI
    ************

Wasu dattijai ne tsaye cikin wasu irin koɗadɗun kaya, wanda kallo ɗaya zaka musu kasan cewa talauci ya samu wurin zama a jikinsu, amma ga mai cikakken hankali kuma kallo ɗaya zaiyi musu yasan cewa dattijaine masu daraja,   dattako, nagarta.
     A guje yaron da suka aika dan yimusu iso da alh ya zuro tare dace musu.
 "Wai yace yana zuwa"
    Sallamar yaron sunkayi da wata koɗaɗɗiyar Murtala data gama koɗewa matuƙa.
   Babu jimawa kuwa Alh Yusuf mai kaba ya iso yana wani ɗage kai sama , shiga tunda wannan ɗan kut bazan dai qarisa ba dai🙀😍 ya faɗa masa zancen auren Nan yakejin bala'i dama kowa ye, kuma yariga yayi alqawarin cewa wannan karon kammm ko waye yazo da buqatar, naiman auren MARYAMU to tabbas kuwa zai bada.
(Tofa wannan shine ana wata ga wata🙀🙀 kome ke shirin faruwa haka ohooo)
     Cikin daraja shi suka shiga gaidashi tare da faɗa masa akan maganar data kawo su, na naimawa ɗansu alfarmarta naiman auren ƴarsa LAYLAH.
Aikuwa babu binciken komi  yace ya basu a turo, cike da mamakin wasu iyayen masu hali irin na Alh Yusuf suke kallon sa.

  Cike da matsanancin tsoro Iliya shola Ameobi ke kallon su lauwali, domin yasan karon ba daɗi.
Wani irin shegen kallo da lauwali ya wurga masa ne yasa shi qara mannewa guri ɗaya, shi kuwa yaya lauwali cike da tacewa a rashin mutunci, ya sake dubun iliya shola Ameobi yace.
   
"Mene nace maka wato duk abinda na maka a banza kenan baka janye batun auren ƙanwas ba ko?"
 Cikin tuno irin baƙar izayar da yasha a wancen loqacin ne yasa Iliyan wani irin zazzaro idanunsa waje, tare da fara magana cikin kiɗima haɗi da tsoro yace.
   "Na ranshe da Allah na janye, daba ai na faɗa maka ita ladiyon ai itace ta bani kuɗi tace in aureta, kuma tun ranar da da kabani kashedin nan nace wa babanku na fasa auren ƴarsan!"
   
    Ya faɗi maganar yana sauƙe ajiyar zuciya don ganin kamar ya tsira.

Shi kuwa lauwali wani irin murmushin mugunta ya jefa ilya dashi tare da faɗin.
"Duk da haka sai an ɗan baka sadaka" 
    Gaf ya buga kan iliya da jikin bishiyar da suke tsaye, yaja tawagarsa suka qara gaba.

    *JALAAL*
 Zaune yake akan ɗaya daga cikin lumtsuna lumtsuman kujerun office ɗin sa, cike da  zallar farin ciki yake gudanar da ayyukansa cikin nishaɗi, domin tunda baffanninsa suka shaida mashi cewar lallei mahaifin yarinyar ya amince da batun auren su, da gaba ɗaya RAYUWAR yakejin kansa cikin wani irin matsanancin farin ciki, haɗi da jin wata irin nutsuwar da hakima baiji irin taba.
 Yana a wannan yanayin ne  kuma ya jiyo qarar wayarsa, cike da shauƙi ya janyo wayar nashi, yana mannata da kunnensa ba tare da ya duba mai qiran ba.
Kasan cewar yana a yanayin shauqine.
     Jin Muryar Fadeelah ne cike da kausasa kalamanta ta shiga faɗin.
    "Yanxu ka kyauta min kenàn, zakayi wannan uwar tafiyar ba tare da ko ka faɗamin ba?, bani da wani matsayi ko martaba kenan a wurin ka JALAAL!"
   Jalaal kuwa zamansa ya gyara tare da ce mata.
    "Duk yadda kika ɗaukana duk ɗaya ne Fadeelah, ina Nima bana da wannan matsayin awurin ki, aduk sanda kika so ko miye aikata kayanki kike, sannan duk inda zakije bani da kima ko kuma matsayin da zaki tambayi sahalewana,kawai abinda kika dama kike!"
    Ya qarishe maganar cikin suyar zuciya domin ta taso mishi mikin dake ɗankare a cen qasan rushinsa.
    "Ohhhh oh naji malam masifatu Sarkin ƴan qorafi na dukka DUNIYAR, yanxun dai tambayarka nake da gaske kana BAUCHIN!?"
      Ta datse maganar ta hanyar qara tambayar shi, jalaal kuwa ganin tana son ɓata masa farin ciki ne yasa shi datse qiran nata baki ɗaya.
    Yaci gaba da abinda yake.

    ★★★★★★★★★★★
  
   Tsaye take daga bakin zauren gidan nasu tare da wani matashin saurayi, wanda kai tsaye bazaka taɓa gane ainihin fuskar saba. Cikin qasa da murya sosae ya kalleta yana sakin murmushin da yayi masifar tafiya da imaninta, ya furta.
  "Alhamdulillah yau Allah Ubangijii ya cika mini fata da kuma burin dana daɗe, ina fatan samunsa, acikin tsayon rayuwana!"
   Sai kuma yaja numfashi,
Alamar dai maganar irin mai girman nan ce.

  "YARINYAR da kakaf gidan daga ita sai mahaifiyarta ne kaɗai ke qaunata, amma ina raye ace za'a aura mata Iliya ɗan shola shili kafuri kuma !"

    Cikin mazarin jiki da matsananci tsoron ɗan nashi baffa dake ɓoye a bayan ƙyauren ɗakin sane yasa shi fara magana cikin tsoran ɗan nashi.
 "Wannan anyi ɗan shegiyar yaro, samm ganinka babu alkhairi acikin sa"
shifa har mantawa yake da yana da wani ɗa lauwali.
Umma dake zaune har yanzu ce ta miƙe tana fita a ɗakin don xuwa wurin lauwalin da kafa gidan maganar tane kaɗai yake ji, acikin sassanyar muryanta wanda ya qara sanyi fiye da da ta nufo shi tare da faɗin.
 "Haba lawan ayi haquri haka nan mana kowanne bawa yana da nashi irin kaɗɗarar, ita kuma nata a haka yazo mata, idan har wannan aure shine mafi Alkhairinta to Ubangijii ya tabbatar mana shi da Alkhairi!"
   Ganin ita ma  umman yau  tanason ta ɓata masa raine yasa yace mata.
"Taya auren Iliya zai zama Alkhairi umma?, bayan kinsan irin abubuwan da yake aikatawa na rantse idan da wanda ya isa ya ɗaura auren Nan shege nake, idan kuwa anyi to na rantse niba ƊAN HALAK bane SHEGE ne"

   Fuuuuuuuuuuuu ya fice a gidan yana fita kuwa yaransa suka rufa mishi baya.
************
       Zaune yake gaban  ummiensa  cikin tattara dukkan nutsuwarsa zuwa ga mahaifiyar nashi yace.
"Ummie  wannan shine kaɗai alfarmar da zakimin!, kana kamar yadda na faɗa miki haka nakeson abun ya kasance, kuma Ni da kaina zance wurin baba hashimun na sanar dashi!"
  Cikin wani irin yanayi ummien ta kalla tilon yaron nata, kana tace.
  "ABDULJALAAL kana ganin babu wata matsala kuwa, idan har muka ɓullo ta wannan al'amarin!?"
Ta qare zancen tana jin tamkar hakan baiyi mata ba, cikin tabbatar wa yace mata.
"Idan harba ta wannan hanyar ba to kuwa bazan taɓa samun cikin muradina ummienaaaa" 
    Yaja sunan nata, cikin yarda tace na amince maka  ABDULJALAAL"
Cike da tsananin farin ciki marar misaltawa ya rubgume mahaifiyar nashi tare da cewa.
   "Na gode ummiena Ubangijii ya biya ki da gidan aljannatul Firdausi!"

***********
   BAUCHI
    ************

Wasu dattijai ne tsaye cikin wasu irin koɗadɗun kaya, wanda kallo ɗaya zaka musu kasan cewa talauci ya samu wurin zama a jikinsu, amma ga mai cikakken hankali kuma kallo ɗaya zaiyi musu yasan cewa dattijaine masu daraja,   dattako, nagarta.
     A guje yaron da suka aika dan yimusu iso da alh ya zuro tare dace musu.
 "Wai yace yana zuwa"
    Sallamar yaron sunkayi da wata koɗaɗɗiyar Murtala data gama koɗewa matuƙa.
   Babu jimawa kuwa Alh Yusuf mai kaba ya iso yana wani ɗage kai sama , shiga tunda wannan ɗan kut bazan dai qarisa ba dai🙀😍 ya faɗa masa zancen auren Nan yakejin bala'i dama kowa ye, kuma yariga yayi alqawarin cewa wannan karon kammm ko waye yazo da buqatar, naiman auren MARYAMU to tabbas kuwa zai bada.
(Tofa wannan shine ana wata ga wata🙀🙀 kome ke shirin faruwa haka ohooo)
     Cikin daraja shi suka shiga gaidashi tare da faɗa masa akan maganar data kawo su, na naimawa ɗansu alfarmarta naiman auren ƴarsa LAYLAH.
Aikuwa babu binciken komi  yace ya basu a turo, cike da mamakin wasu iyayen masu hali irin na Alh Yusuf suke kallon sa.

  Cike da matsanancin tsoro Iliya shola Ameobi ke kallon su lauwali, domin yasan karon ba daɗi.
Wani irin shegen kallo da lauwali ya wurga masa ne yasa shi qara mannewa guri ɗaya, shi kuwa yaya lauwali cike da tacewa a rashin mutunci, ya sake dubun iliya shola Ameobi yace.
   
"Mene nace maka wato duk abinda na maka a banza kenan baka janye batun auren ƙanwas ba ko?"
 Cikin tuno irin baƙar izayar da yasha a wancen loqacin ne yasa Iliyan wani irin zazzaro idanunsa waje, tare da fara magana cikin kiɗima haɗi da tsoro yace.
   "Na ranshe da Allah na janye, daba ai na faɗa maka ita ladiyon ai itace ta bani kuɗi tace in aureta, kuma tun ranar da da kabani kashedin nan nace wa babanku na fasa auren ƴarsan!"
   
    Ya faɗi maganar yana sauƙe ajiyar zuciya don ganin kamar ya tsira.

Shi kuwa lauwali wani irin murmushin mugunta ya jefa ilya dashi tare da faɗin.
"Duk da haka sai an ɗan baka sadaka" 
    Gaf ya buga kan iliya da jikin bishiyar da suke tsaye, yaja tawagarsa suka qara gaba.

    *JALAAL*
 Zaune yake akan ɗaya daga cikin lumtsuna lumtsuman kujerun office ɗin sa, cike da  zallar farin ciki yake gudanar da ayyukansa cikin nishaɗi, domin tunda baffanninsa suka shaida mashi cewar lallei mahaifin yarinyar ya amince da batun auren su, da gaba ɗaya RAYUWAR yakejin kansa cikin wani irin matsanancin farin ciki, haɗi da jin wata irin nutsuwar da hakima baiji irin taba.
 Yana a wannan yanayin ne  kuma ya jiyo qarar wayarsa, cike da shauƙi ya janyo wayar nashi, yana mannata da kunnensa ba tare da ya duba mai qiran ba.
Kasan cewar yana a yanayin shauqine.
     Jin Muryar Fadeelah ne cike da kausasa kalamanta ta shiga faɗin.
    "Yanxu ka kyauta min kenàn, zakayi wannan uwar tafiyar ba tare da ko ka faɗamin ba?, bani da wani matsayi ko martaba kenan a wurin ka JALAAL!"
   Jalaal kuwa zamansa ya gyara tare da ce mata.
    "Duk yadda kika ɗaukana duk ɗaya ne Fadeelah, ina Nima bana da wannan matsayin awurin ki, aduk sanda kika so ko miye aikata kayanki kike, sannan duk inda zakije bani da kima ko kuma matsayin da zaki tambayi sahalewana,kawai abinda kika dama kike!"
    Ya qarishe maganar cikin suyar zuciya domin ta taso mishi mikin dake ɗankare a cen qasan rushinsa.
    "Ohhhh oh naji malam masifatu Sarkin ƴan qorafi na dukka DUNIYAR, yanxun dai tambayarka nake da gaske kana BAUCHIN!?"
      Ta datse maganar ta hanyar qara tambayar shi, jalaal kuwa ganin tana son ɓata masa farin ciki ne yasa shi datse qiran nata baki ɗaya.
    Yaci gaba da abinda yake.

  "Alhamdulillah yau Allah Ubangijii ya cika mini fata da kuma burin dana daɗe, ina fatan samunsa, acikin tsayon rayuwana!"

 ★★★★★★★★

Ahankali yaɗan ware lumsassun Idanunsa kaɗan tare da laluben wayar nashi, cikin barcin da bai gama sakin saba cenñnn qarshen gado ya jiyota, hakan yasa ya janyo ya anutse yana amsa qiran kai tsaye ba tare da yasan waye mai qiran ba.
A ƙalla yakusa ɗaukan 20 minute da wayar a kunnensa, sai kuma kawai ya rumtse idanunsa da qarfin masifa, kafin jikinsa ya ɗauki wani irin karkarwa tamkar mai rawar ɗari, tuni idanunsa ya sanja launi daga farare xuwa jawur, gaba ɗaya jijiyoyin kansa kuwa sun tashi sunyi raɗa².
      Ba tare danaji  mene aka faɗamar daga cikin wayar ba, amma dai da alamun koma mene aka gaya masa mai girma ne.

Wani irin zazzafan numfashi ya fesar  sabo da  jin kalaman na barƙa dukkan wata  lakar dake jikinsa, nan yaji wani irin abu  na sauƙa tun daga ƙwaƙwalwarsa har xuwa tafin qafafunsa, inda gaba ɗaya yaji jikin sa yayi wani irin sanyin da tunda yake bai taɓa jin koda kwatankwacin hakan ba. Fesar da numfashi yayi  mai zafi tare da zare wayar a kunnensa yana jifa da ita baki ɗayan, kansa ya tallafo ta hanyar dafesu da dukka hannuwansa biyun  wani irin ja idanunsa sukayi ga kuma wani ruwa ruwa da suka tara suna ƙallin wahayen da suka taran masa. Shi kansa bazaice ga yadda yake ji acikin RAI da kuma RUHINSA saba, kawai abinda ya sani zuciyarsa tayi masa wani irin nauyin da yake tunanin ko haɗiyar yawu bazai taɓa haɗiyewa ba, cike da matsanancin tashin hankali marar misaltawa kana cikin galabaituwa ya fara jero dukkan adda'oin da suka mashi.

  "Ya ilahi ya mujibud da'awati, ya hayyu ya qayyum bi rahma tika astaghisu, la haula wala quwwata illa billah!"

 Haka yayi ta duk wata Adda'ar ta tazo bakinsa, faɗar irin tashin hankalin da yake ciki ma faɗa loƙacine, amma kai tsaye bazaka taɓa fuskantar halin da yake aciki ba, kasancewarsa mutum mai taurin zuciya yasa sammm farat ɗaya ba zaka taɓa fuskantar yanayin da yake aciki ba.
      Sai kuma ya shiga sakin ajiyar zuciya akai akai alamun zuciyarsa ta saussauta daga irin zafin da take yimar, ahankali ya shafa sajen dake kwance a kuncinsa, wanda yake fidda wani irin shining ɗin kyau da iyakar gyaran da yake sha.

   ←←←→→→
 Cikin matsanancin kuka take faɗin.
    "Don Allah! don darajar  Annabi Muhammad Sallallahu alaihi Wasallama baffa kayi haƙuri kada  ka auramin  Iliya!.
    Na yarda ka auramin koma waye amma banda Iliya, baffa Iliya ɗan shaye shaye ne manemin mata ne nasan kasan hakan, baffa koda makaho ko kuturu ka auramin Wallahi na yarda zan rayu dashi amma don darajar fiyayyen halitta banda Iliya!, baffa kasan waye Iliya kuwa? mutumin daya kasance, You are a drunkard, you are looking for women and an infidel who does not pray, na haɗaka da GIRMAN UBANGIJIIN sammai bakwai kayi haƙuri baffa!

 Ta karashe maganar 
   Cikin matsanancin kuka, laylerh take roƙôn mahaifin nata, shi kuwa cikin ainihin gaskiyarsa yace mata.
  
           "Saidai kiyi haƙuri MARYAMU aure nekam babu fashi insha Allah, domin na gaji da ganin ki haka Nan na gaji idan na barkin waye zaki aura? Yaushe kuma zakiyi AUREN? To Ni gaskiya na gaji da ciyar da balagaggiya kamarki  ke wai ko haushin kanki baki jine kammmm!, ki duba fa ki gani gaba ɗaya ƙannenki duk sunyi aure daga masu ƴaƴa biyu sai masu ƴaƴa uku don haka kuwa dole ne na aura miki Iliya!'
     Ya qare maganar yana shigewa cikin ɗakinsa .

Inna ladiyo da hajja kuwa   farin cikin duniya ya gaba isarsu, tabbas boka yayi gaskiya domin dama yace musu zasuga irin MIJIN da MARYAM ɗin zata aura, kana yace musu koda wasa babu wani mahalukin daya isa yazo wurinta da sunan zance. 
{Kunji shegu sun manta cewar  Allah shine mai kowa mai komai}
   
A tare suka tafa tare da sakin shewa, sai kuma suka ɗauki guda irin ta ƴan duniyar nan tare da fara yada wasu baƙaƙen maganganu marasa daɗinji.
Umma kuwa dake zaune a cikin ɗakinta murmushi mai ciwo ta saki, tare da ci gaba da karatun Alqur'ani mai girma da take karantawa. Kamar kazar da kwai ya fashewa haka laylerh ta shigo ɗakin tare da zama kusa da umman, tana zamewa ta ɗora kanta bisa qafafun umman nata, cikin son ta kwantarma da yarinyarta hankali ta shiga lallashinta, tare da ce mata taci gaba da adda'a insha Allah tanaji acikin jikinta Allah zai musanya mata da mafi Alkhairinsa, haka dai tayi ta  mata nasihohi masu ratsa zuciya a qarshene kuma ta ɗago idanun laylan tace mata.

*"Kiyi haƙuri Maryam! ƙarshen haƙuri nasarace, ƙarshen tsanani darajace, ƙarshen wahala ɗaukakace, ƙarshen  jarabawa kuma Alkhairi ne, don haka ki kasance cikin yaƙini da kuma godiya ga Allah"*


"Baffa! baffalo!! Baffalota!!!"

"Wai kana ina neeeeee  nasan kana jina, idan an isa ayi wannan auren shege nake laylan za'a aurawa Iliya!, Iliyan da kowa ya sani mugun ɓarawone! Ɗan daba!!   Inna ladiyo kuwa da tunda taji shigowar ɗan nata ta shige qarqashinsu gadon ko ƙwaƙƙwaran motsi bata kuma yiba, saida taji yana zabba mata wannan uban qiran ne kuma bata san sanda ta saki fitsarin azabar tsora ba.

    "Ohhhh oh naji malam masifatu Sarkin ƴan qorafi na dukka DUNIYAR, yanxun dai tambayarka nake da gaske kana BAUCHIN!?"
      Ta datse maganar ta hanyar qara tambayar shi, jalaal kuwa ganin tana son ɓata masa farin ciki ne yasa shi datse qiran nata baki ɗaya.
    Yaci gaba da abinda yake.

    ★★★★★★★★★★★
  
   Tsaye take daga bakin zauren gidan nasu tare da wani matashin saurayi, wanda kai tsaye bazaka taɓa gane ainihin fuskar saba. Cikin qasa da murya sosae ya kalleta yana sakin murmushin da yayi masifar tafiya da imaninta, ya furta.
  "Alhamdulillah yau Allah Ubangijii ya cika mini fata da kuma burin dana daɗe, ina fatan samunsa, acikin tsayon rayuwana!"
   Sai kuma yaja numfashi,
Alamar dai maganar irin mai girman nan ce.
 Ita kuwa qasa tayi da kanta sosae jin zuciyarta na wani irin beating ahankali ta soma murza hannunwanta, sai kuma ta sake ɗago kanta a karo na ba adadi da qara dubansa, idanunsa idanuwansa tabbas idanunsa suna mata kama da wasu idanuwan data kasa mance su tsayin rayuwarta, ta manta jikinsa fuskarsa amma ta kasa mance idanuwansa tsayon rayuwarta, ahankali ta sake ɗago kanta zuwa gare shi.
   A take gabanta ya sake yankewa ya faɗi tare da wani irin bugu da zuciyarta yake mata, bakinta ne ya shiga mamul mamul da alama tana son furta wata maganar ne, amma bakin nata yaƙi bata haɗin kai ta hanyar yi mata nauyi.
   A daidai wannan loƙacin kuma Inna ladiyo ta fito daga cikin gidansu tare da qarewa mutumin dake tsaye kallo,sai kuma ta kwashe da wata uwar dariya, ganin wani irin kayan dake jikinsa wani koɗaɗɗan yadine irin ishurunka marayan Nan da yaga ji da koɗewa matuƙa, ga wani shafta shaftan takalman da suka wa qafarsa yawa inda qafar take sanye cikin wata yagwalalliyar Safa, sai kuma ta sake ɗago kanta ganin fuskarsa duk irin tamburan tabo ne, ga kuma wata uwar yaƙunanniyar farar rigar daya sanya irin wannan mai kwalar mai dogon hannu gaba ɗaya ya gama fita a hayyacinsa, hakanne yasa ta kuma kwashewa da wata uwar dariyar tare da soma kwala qiran sunan Hajja.

   "Hajja! hajja!! hajjaaa!!!"
Hakka kuwa data ke tsaya daga bakin zauren ce ta fito wajen, ganin Inna ladiyon nata ƙaƙata dariya ne yasa ta duban abinda take ma dariyar, ai kuwa itama atake ta kwashe da uwar dariyar datafi na Inna ladiyon, tare da nuna wanda yake tsayen da hannunta.

   Inna ladiyo datake dariyar muguntane ta dakata tare da faɗin.

   Hajja ce tayi caraf ta hanyar cafe zancen tare da soma faɗin.
     "Ohhhhhhh ashe haka zamu sha biki kuma, naji malam na cewa bikin bazai wuce nan da sati mai zuwa"

   "To bawan Allah Ubangijii ya baka ikon zama da GUZUMAR KARFA kana  UWAR MATA wacce ta gama gantalewa a saman kwalta guntun wasu ƙattin"
    Ta faɗi maganar da gayya.

   Shi kuwa wannan bawan Allahn tuni jikin sa ya shiga wani irin tsuma haɗi da mazari tamkar wanda kejin ɗari, cikin wani mugun yanayi ya ɗago kansa zuwa garesu.

Ahankali yaɗan ware lumsassun Idanunsa kaɗan tare da laluben wayar nashi, cikin barcin da bai gama sakin saba cenñnn qarshen gado ya jiyota, hakan yasa ya janyo ya anutse yana amsa qiran kai tsaye ba tare da yasan waye mai qiran ba.
A ƙalla yakusa ɗaukan 20 minute da wayar a kunnensa, sai kuma kawai ya rumtse idanunsa da qarfin masifa, kafin jikinsa ya ɗauki wani irin karkarwa tamkar mai rawar ɗari, tuni idanunsa ya sanja launi daga farare xuwa jawur, gaba ɗaya jijiyoyin kansa kuwa sun tashi sunyi raɗa².
      Ba tare danaji  mene aka faɗamar daga cikin wayar ba, amma dai da alamun koma mene aka gaya masa mai girma ne.

Wani irin zazzafan numfashi ya fesar  sabo da  jin kalaman na barƙa dukkan wata  lakar dake jikinsa, nan yaji wani irin abu  na sauƙa tun daga ƙwaƙwalwarsa har xuwa tafin qafafunsa, inda gaba ɗaya yaji jikin sa yayi wani irin sanyin da tunda yake bai taɓa jin koda kwatankwacin hakan ba. Fesar da numfashi yayi  mai zafi tare da zare wayar a kunnensa yana jifa da ita baki ɗayan, kansa ya tallafo ta hanyar dafesu da dukka hannuwansa biyun  wani irin ja idanunsa sukayi ga kuma wani ruwa ruwa da suka tara suna ƙallin wahayen da suka taran masa. Shi kansa bazaice ga yadda yake ji acikin RAI da kuma RUHINSA saba, kawai abinda ya sani zuciyarsa tayi masa wani irin nauyin da yake tunanin ko haɗiyar yawu bazai taɓa haɗiyewa ba, cike da matsanancin tashin hankali marar misaltawa kana cikin galabaituwa ya fara jero dukkan adda'oin da suka mashi.

  "Ya ilahi ya mujibud da'awati, ya hayyu ya qayyum bi rahma tika astaghisu, la haula wala quwwata illa billah!"

To Ni gaskiya na gaji da ciyar da balagaggiya kamarki  ke wai ko haushin kanki baki jine kammmm!, ki duba fa ki gani gaba ɗaya ƙannenki duk sunyi aure daga masu ƴaƴa biyu sai masu ƴaƴa uku don haka kuwa dole ne na aura miki Iliya!'

Kamar kazar da kwai ya fashewa haka laylerh ta shigo ɗakin tare da zama kusa da umman, tana zamewa ta ɗora kanta bisa qafafun umman nata, cikin son ta kwantarma da yarinyarta hankali ta shiga lallashinta, tare da ce mata taci gaba da adda'a insha Allah tanaji acikin jikinta Allah zai musanya mata da mafi Alkhairinsa, haka dai tayi ta  mata nasihohi masu ratsa zuciya a qarshene kuma ta ɗago idanun laylan tace mata.

shifa har mantawa yake da yana da wani ɗa lauwali.

    *JALAAL*
 Zaune yake akan ɗaya daga cikin lumtsuna lumtsuman kujerun office ɗin sa, cike da  zallar farin ciki yake gudanar da ayyukansa cikin nishaɗi, domin tunda baffanninsa suka shaida mashi cewar lallei mahaifin yarinyar ya amince da batun auren su, da gaba ɗaya RAYUWAR yakejin kansa cikin wani irin matsanancin farin ciki, haɗi da jin wata irin nutsuwar da hakima baiji irin taba.

*Washe garin ranan da safe*
★★★★★★

Tsaye umma take abakin gadon da laylan take kwance, ahankali ta shiga motsa idanunta alamun tana son farkawa kenan.
Da sauri umman ta kuma matsowa gare ta taimaka mata tayi ta samu ta tashi, a bakin toilet ta tsaya ita kuma ta shige cike tare da rufowa qofar.
Bayan ta idar da sallarne ta kishin gida da jikin gadon, ahankali dr.deeja ta tura qofar tare da shigowa, da fara'a a fuskarta ta shiga gaida umman, amsata umman tayi da kulawa.

Ahankali ya turo qafar ɗakin tare da kafe Layla da ido domin da ita ya fara cin karo, kanta ta samu da da kasa cire idanunta dake cikin nasa.
Qarasa shigowa yayi tare da fara gaida umman,nan ya shiga gaidai umman cike da girmamawa, da fara'a a fuskarta ta shiga amsa shi, tare da yi masa godiyar nauyin da suka ɗaura masa, qasa yayi da kansa sosae domin bazai iya ci gaba da kallon cikin idanuwanta ba.
"Umma zan maidaku gida, domin Dr Deejah tace ta baku sallama"

Haka Umma ta shiga gode masa, inda shi kuwa ya miƙe tsamm tare da ya fita yana cewa su fito don Allah, daga cen qasan ranshi yanajin daɗin Adda'ar ta gareshi, sosai yaji matar da burgeshi fiye da tunanin mai karatu.

Yau 10/1/ 2024 daya kama ranar juma'atu babbar rana kenan
Aka ɗaura auren jalaal Areef tare da amaryarsa Maryam Yusuf mai kaba, Akan sadaki Naira dubu ɗari biyar tare da mukullin mota. Ɗaurin auren daya samu halartar manyan mutane manyan ƴan kasuwa manyan ƴan siyasa harda mai girma shugaban qasar yanxun dake, wanda aka naɗa bayan rasuwar mahaifinsa, Alhaji AREEFILLAHI MUHD MAI NASARA keñan.
   Wanda hakan ya girgiza jama'a da dama, Alh Yusuf mai kaba kuwa ai suman tsaye ne yayi, domin da farko da yaga irin manyan mutane da kuma irin manyan motocin dake fakin a bakin babban masallacin juma'ar Bauchin, yasha ɗaurin auren biyu ne sai sai kuma gashi yaji abinda ya kusan girgiza duniyarsa, domin dakam ma ko harabar masallacin bai nufa fa saida su baffan jalaal waɗan nan tsoffin biyu da suka je newama jalaal auren ƴarsa ne suka kamo hannayensa suna shiga cikin masallacin direct, inda suma ɗin da bai wani gane suba.     A guje ya zugo ganin mutum kwance yana barci kashirɓan, loƙacin yayi daidai da shigowar wata jibgeciyar mota harabar gidan, jalaal shi da Messi dake cikin motar me sukayi saurin fitowa suna tambayar ko lafiya, cikin haki yake faɗin "Wallahi mutum, aljanine acikin bayi na!"
  "Subhanallahi aljani Kuma a ina muje cewar jalaal Messi dake da masifar tsoro ne, ganin jalaal ɗin ya shige gaba ne yasa shi saurin barin gurin yana yin  ɓangarensu.

Turus jalaal yaja ya tsaya ganin mahaifin matarsa ne, da ummie ta shedamar cewar ba'a ganshi, kwance lake lake acikin toilet. Cikin sauri ya qara shiga tara da fara cewa " baffa! Baffa!! Baffa!!!" Baffa yusif kuwa jin kamar ana  magana ne yasa saurin tashi zaune yana faɗin.
   " Wallahi babu inda zan tafi, ai dama ana yiwa amarya zaman burganci, to wallahi nine zan yiwa ƴata zaman burgancin da kaina, da kuka naimo nima babu inda zan tafi aini naga wajen zama"
    "Innalillahi wa'inna ilair raji'un, innalillahi wa'inna ilair raji'un "
  Shine kalmar da jalaal yake maimaituwa,, ganin surukinsa guda da wannan aika aikar gashi ma a gaban qaninshi shine da mai gaɗi, dama ace shi qaɗaine ai da dama dama.

BAUCHI
      
Alhamdulillah su adda fatsima sunje gida lafiya qalau, saidai batun baffa yusif daya ɓata ba'a ganshi ba, koda jin wannan lamari Nan take gaban umma ya faɗi acen qasan ranta ta ringa nanata innalillahi wa'inna ilair raji'un, domin tasan tabbas malam yusif ɗin bawai ɓatan yayi ba kamar yacce suka gaya mata ɗin.

  "Innalillahi Ciki kuma likita?   Ɗan zama bakinta tayi alaman tana son tayi magana azafafe ya kuma tura bakinsa cikin bakinta yana kamo harshen ta.

Ya faɗa cikin wata iriyar muryarn dake shaking aloƙacin da tongue ɗinsa ya samu shiga cikin bakinta kuwa,wani irin abu yaji tun daga tsakar kansa har zuwa tsaƙiyar tafin qafarsa, sucking  lips and tongue nata ya shiga yi cikin wani qayataccen salo na musamman.
Hakan ne kuma yasa ya fara fitar da wani irin breathing dake bayyana tsantsar peelings ɗinta gareshi.
     Idanunta da sukayi rau rau suke shirin fidda ruwa ta zuba mishi, rungumar daya mata ya gyara mata tare da ɗan wara idanunsa akan, wanda suka ɗan sanja kolo daga farare zuwa jajaye.
    "La Mia vita"
   Ya qira sunanta cikin wata iriyar matacciyar murya.
Cikin wani irin masifaffen yanayin da ta samu akan acikine ya sata qara shigewa cikin jikinsa tare da ƙamƙameshi tana ɗaura kanta saman qircinsa bayan ta samu qwace kanta daga gare shi, ajihar zuciya ya shiga saki, domin bai taɓa samun kusanci kamar haka daga gare ta ba, jin yadda wani irin abu ke qoqarin taso masa ne ya sanya shi saurin zare jikinsa daga cikin jikinta yana barin kitchen ɗin baki ɗaya.

  A kasalance ta qarasa kimtsa kitchen ɗin saida ta gama  gyara ko ina na sashin nata kafin taje tayo wanka ta shirya kanta cikin dojuwar riga harp bubu tare da feshe jikinta ta tularen ta masu azabar ƙamshi.
   Kai tsaye bedroom nashi ta nufa, Knoking tayi jin shiru ne kuma ya sanya ta tura qofar tare da shiga cikin bedroom ɗin, wani azababben qamshin sane ya mata sallama idanunta da har yanxun suke Nan   taɗan ware tare fesar da numfashi.
   Ganin kamar ɗakin a ɗan harmutse yake ne yasanya cikin sauri sauri gudu gudu ta shiga gyaran ɗakin, cikin qanqanin loƙaci ta gama shirya komai tuni ɗakin ya shiga wani irin shining tare da daddaɗan roomfreshner ɗin data feshe ɗakin dashi.

Ɗaure da towel ya fito akan qugunsa, ɗaya hannunsa kuma wani mini towel ne yana tsane tulin sumar dake kwance luff luff saman kansa, sai fidda wani irin dauwamammeen qamshi take da shining na gyaran da take sha.
 Wani irin lumshe idanun sa yayi sabo da wani ni'imantaccen ƙamshim daya shaqa kafin ya ware lumsassun idanunsa 
            Ahankali ya sauqe ganinsa akan ta dake tsaye gaban mirror da dukkan alamu nan take qarasa gyara mishi, Ajiyar zuciya ya sauƙe yana ɗan fesa mata  ruwan dake bakinsa.
  A bazata ta juyo ganinsa da tayi ɗaure da towel ne yasan yata rumtse idanunta gammmm  tare da saurin juyawa, da nufin barin ɗakin.
Taku ɗaya a na biyu ya cimma ta qugunta ya damƙo tare da mannata da nashi jikin, acikin kunnenta ya raɗa mata.
    "Wifey Ina zakije kuma?, bayan gama qarasa cikata aikin ladar naki ba, muje ki shirya Ni"

  Ta shirya shi kuma

Ta faɗi maganar tana turo ɗan ƙaramin bakinta gaba.
"Indai akan kine sammm na yarda banda kunyar LAYLERH!"
  Haka taji sauqar muryar sa amma wannan mutumin da shegen kunnen masifa yake.
  Ɗan murmushi kawai ya saki domin tsabbb ya kuma jin abinda ta faɗa ɗin, kawai qyale ta yayi dan baya wani son ya cika takura mata, yanason tafi haka sakin jiki dashi.
"Idan ka  gama shiryawa food is ready " tana faɗar hakan tayi saurin sauqa gudun kada ya kuma janyo mata wani aika aikar.
Sauri sauri gudu gudu ya gama kimtsa kansa  cikin wasu haɗaɗɗun riga da wando na comfy white color irin mai salo salon nan  sai t-shirt ɗin rigarsa inda ita kuma ta kasance ruwan qasa mai manyan salo.
   Tularen dolcy and Gabbana ya feshe jikinsa dashi, directly ya nufi cikin qaton parlourn nasu, tana zaune da cup ma milk shake riqe a hannun ta ahankali take ɗan sipping na limon sai ɗan lumsassun idanunta take, wanda eyelashes ɗinta suka kwanta luff akan kyakkyawar fuskarta, 
Wani irin abu ya haɗiye tare da ɗan daidai ta nutsuwar sa, zama yayi akan ɗaya daga cikin kujerun dining table ɗin.
   Ahankali ta ajje cup ɗin tare da janyo Wormers ɗin gabanta, plate ta fitar dare da fara saving nashi, tun kamin yaji testing abincin yaji yawon bakinsa yana tsinƙewa gabansa ta tura masa tare da cewa "Bismillah"
 Kansa kawai ya iya kaɗa mata tare da jawo plate ɗin gabansa.
    "Ya ilahi" 
  Ya faɗi lokacin daya kai lomar farko bakinsa, sabili da wani irin daddaɗan daɗin dayaji, sai kuma ya ɗago kansa  yana zuba mata su
anutse ya shiga  lodama cikin sa lafiyayyan ganimar daya daɗe bai kwasa ba.
By 11.am suka kammala karin kumallon shi yayi sama ita kuma tayi kitchen, lafiyayyan tuwon semo da miyar egushin data haɗawa ummien ta ɗauka direct part ɗin ummie ta nufa.
   Da sallama ta shiga parlourn ummien tana zaune akan sopa laptop riqe a hannun ta, sallamar ta amsa  yana dawo da gaba attention ɗinta kan laylan, cike da girmamawa yace "UMMIE Barka da safiya fatan kina lafiya"

  Wani irin daɗi ne ya ziyarci  ummien loqaci guda tare da JALAAL dake tsaye daga bakin qofar shigowa amma bai kai ga qarasa shigowar ba,
   Cike da matsanancin SO ƘAUNA gami da burgewa UMMIE da JALAAL suka shiga binta dashi.
    Domin wannan shine na farko da wani ya taɓa dafo abu ya kawo mata, hakan ya sanya ta qarajin soyayya mai qarfi na ratsa ta ta LAYLERHN.

Ji yake tamkar yazo ya ɗagata sama yayi ta juyi da ita tsabar  wani irin maɗaukakin farin cikin da yake a ciki, ji yake wata iriyar soyayyarta na qara ratsa duk wata gaɓa mai jini dake cikin jikinsa.
Yana ina? Yana me har tayi wannan hidima haaka kana ko afuska bata nuna masa tayi girki da mahaifiyarsa ba, har suka kammala cin abincisu kuwa saima data bari yabar wurin kana ta ɗakko abincin ta kawoma ummien sa.wasu narkakkun abubuwane masu masifar qarfin gaske yaji yana kuma ratsa shi  a game da ita.
Hannuwansa  dake sarqe ya zubesu xuwa cikin aljihun dogon wandon comfy ɗin dake jikinsa yana zuba mata lumsassun idanunsa, masu kama da yana jin barci.

Inba haka ba wllh mai rabani dake sai Allah"

*Association of Nigeria construction companies*

   A coumpaund na tsakiyar Companyn suka faka motocin, inda manya motoci suke Nan a pappake da dukkan alamu wani  muhimman abune suke shirin yi. Ɗaya daga cikin guards nashi ne ya fito tare da saurin buɗe masa motar, tare da sara masa.

Cikin takun ƙasaita dajin ya isa ya ratsa tsakiyar babban ɗakin meeting ɗin, direct kujerar dake nan babu kowa akai ya nufa, yana zama cikin izza tare da kuma tsuƙe kyakkyawar fuskarsa.

  Amma dole babu yacce ya iya haka ya haqura da haka zaman meeting ɗin nasu ya tashi, wanda babban meeting ɗin masu kuma yana cenñnn qasar EGYPT.

Sha BIYUn dare jirgin su ya sauqa

 A NATALYA'S HOTEL suka sauqa wanda tun kamin yaje ya gama musu buking na komai. Tamkar rana haka garin yake tsabar haske kai baka cema ba rana bace.

 Da wani irin mugun sauri jalaal ya tallafo ta xuwa jikinsa  da idanunsa ya bita wanda suka sanja launi xuwa jajaye, saurin rungumota yayi yana manneta cikin jikinsa yana  jin bugun k'irjin ta  a hankali yake furta  "Ki daina kuka  wifey Crying is more important than my heart and soul because of the situation I am in!." 
Ya ƙarashe maganar yana mai ƙurama waje guda firgitattun idanunsa,  shi kaɗai yake imagining kalan azaban da zai baiwa su Fahad  da kuma messi musamman ma dai Messi da second by second kalaman da ya faɗ akan laylerhn sa ke dawo masa, sosae maganar  tayi hurting ɗinsa, yama rasa wanne kalan azabar zai bama fahad ɗin.

 Ita kuwa Layla ganin yadda hannunsa ke zubar da jini ne ya sanyata qoqarin tashi, sai kuma jiri ya ɗebeta,ba kaɗan ba hankalin ta ya tashi shine dalilin daya sanyata fashewa da kuka kawai, kwantar da kanta yayi saman jikinsa,
      A zahiri idonsa a lumshe suke, amma kuma abaɗini yana kallon Messi ne daya naɗe jikin  mahaifiyarsa, shi kansa shaida ne Messi yana da tsananin  uban tsoro kamar farar kura.

Watan queen biyar a duniya whohoho zoka ga kuruwa wajen Hajj Mama, haka ta ringa tunballonta ita kaɗai, tana cewa ita dama tasan wannan jarababben yaron da wuri zai qara dirkawa ƴar mutane ciki, ummie kuwa baiwar Allah sai toshe kunnawanta tayi.
    Ita kanta ogar saida akayi artabu sosea da ita domin har saida ta kaisu ga ummanta kana ta haƙura domin yanda cewa tayi sai an fitar da shi, 


AFTER One  years.

  Cikin shekara gudan abubuwa da dama sun faru masu daɗi da marasa daɗi. Inda aka fito dasu Mum fadeelah da momin Nafisa bayan baqar azabar da suka sha, daga oga maleek toufiq, cikin sanyi jiki da Sanin Allah ɗaya yake suka nemi yafiyar ummie inda tace ita kamm dama basu taɓa mata komai ba, nafisa qiri da muzu taki komawa wurin mahaifiyarta.
  Haka taci gaba da rayuwa gaban ummie, inda Messi Jalal ya maida shi bakin aikinsa, aikuwa qyaral ya qyalla ido yaga dije yace Nan duniya yaga matar aure.
       Cike da farin ciki da jin dadi maganar takai ga baffa da Inna ladiyo aikuwa Nan take baffa yace duk yadda ummie da Jalal suka yanke shine daidai tuni akayi bikin su, wanda zuwa wannan loqacin dije tayi wani irin kilewa da wankewa,  gefen nafisa tsananin ƙaunar yaya lauwali ne ya sanya ta a gaba amma ta gaza furtama kowa, haka tayi ta dokan soyayyarsa ba tare da kowa ya sani ba. Kuma loqacin ne Fahad ya dawo mata tace sammmm har gaban abadan bata qara fatan su kuma haɗa inuwa ɗaya dashi, haka ummie ta matsa mata kan ta fidda miji ta gaji da ganinta haka nan. Ta shiga tashin hankalin hakan  gashi kunyar cewa lauwali take so  take, cikin wata qudira ta Ubangijii kuma yaya lauwali ya gabatar ma da jalaal cewa shifa yana son nafisa da aure idan har wani bai riga shi ba.
   Don tsabar farin ciki kuwa Nafisa harda zuwa ruwa akan tiles tasha lokacin da ummie take faɗa maganar da Jalal in ya tuntuɓe ta dashi.
           Haka aka sanya ranar aurensu wata huɗu ciff, haka suka shiga soyewarsu ita Lawan Yusuf mai kaba yaya lauwali ada.
      Ana saura sati biyu bikin su Nafisa Layla ta haihu inda ta haifi ƴaƴa biyu duk maza aikuwa Jalal yace dole yasan ya sunan baffa kodan ladan soyayyar daya nuna masa harda su ɓuya a banɗakin mai gadi, sai a nan ne ma Layla take jin diramar baffanta da Jalal aikuwa tayi dariya kamar cikin zai yi ciwo.
inda yaran suka ci sunan mahaifin Jalal wato  muhd Areefullah da sunan baffan su Layla Yusuf .
Areefullah suna qiransa da  Sultan  shi kuma Yusuf ana qiransa da na'eem, haka ranar bikin su yaya lauwali tazo su Layla aka sha shagalin biki, inda yake zaune a Anan cikin abujan, tunda anan yake aikinsa.
   Tuni Inna ladiyo ta saduda ta zubar da komai dama muguwar hassada ce kuma alhamdulillah Allah ya yaye mata itama yanxu saidai ta taima wosu, sosae amaren suke shan soyayyarsu cikin aminci da kwanciyar hankali. Aknan kuwa wani ɗan yayar maman tane mai suna fauzan ya aure ta wanda baki ɗayansu ukun duk Jalal ne ya musu komai dun daga kama kan kayan daki kitchen daidai sauran abubuwa shine ya musu. 
   Fadeelah kuwa makarantar islamiyya ta koma  ta yaki da jahilci ta matan aure, sosai ta gane ba qaramin kuskure ta tafka a rayuwan aurenta ba, inda har gida itama tazo ta bama ummie haƙuri tare da Jalal, duk suka yafe mata sukace komai ya wuce, da ummie ta tuntuɓi Jalal da maganar ko zai maida fadeelahn qiri2 ya fito ya gaya mata cewar gaskiya koya maida ita cutar ta zai yi. Domin yasan bazai taɓa iya misalta adalci a tsakanin su ba.
    Haka ummie ta haƙura da Hajj Mama taji zancen na ummie kuwa haka ta qare mata tanaji da tujararta.

*Bayan shekaru ashirin da biyu*
  
Abubuwa da dama sun faru ciki kuwa harda nutuwar Hajj Mama, wacce ta girgiza duniyar su Layla da ummie dama duk wani masoyin wannan ahalin.
  Zuwa loqacin yaran Alhaji abduljalaal Areefullahi bakwai domin sau  biyu Layla tana ƴan biyu bayan Sultan da na'eem sai da ta haifi mai sunan ummie wacce suke qira da mamie kafin ta qara wasu ƴan biyun daya mace ɗaya namiji.
  Muhd Naseer wanda suke qira shi da nasreen, sai kuma Maryam wacce suke qira da laylerh inda yama rayuwarsa takwara kuma yace itama da laylerhn za'a ka  qiranta.
          Sai  Sadeeq sai kuma auta afreen. Soyayyar duniya tana kan afreen kasancewarta AUTA qare nono.

Gaba ɗaya ahalin gidan  suna tattare  makeken parlourn gidan baki ɗaya kasancewar yau weekend ne, afreen ce data kasance ƴar kimanin shekaru biyar zaune ɗare² ta kanainaye jikin papieynta, cike da tsananin shogoɓa tace.
    "Nidai yau ina gidan mamiey Aknan"
Tayi Maganar tana qara turo ɗan cukulon bakinta gaba, kafin little laylerh itama tace. "Ni kuma yau ina tare da papiey naaa da  sweetheart Ammeynmu".
         Sai yanxu wata matashiyar budurwar dake hakimce sama sopa idanunta sanye da wani farin gilashi irin na likitocin Nan ta sami damar ciro kanta, ya subhanallahi lallei tsarki ya tabbata ga Allah subhanahu wata'ala da ya qagi wannan halittar mai tsananin kama da mahaifinta sakkk. Cikin muryarnta mara son hayaniya tace.
   "Do Allah du kuma mutane shiru, a ahhh!, ba kuganni a zaune  bane kuma kunsan idan ina wuri samm banson hayaniya ba!"
   Tayi Maganar cike da qosawa da rashin son maganar ta tare da ɗan lumshe idanunta, alamun bata so har tayi magana mai girman haka ba.
kurrrrr papieyn nata ya qure ta da ido exactly komai nata irin nashi ne take yi rashin son maganar ta, rashin hayaniyar ta, kai hatta yadda yake lumshe idanunsa ya kuma bude su haka yake.
 Wani irin daddaɗan qamshin dayaji ne ya sanya shi ɗago kansa xuwa saitin inda yaji qamshin na tashi. 
   Tsaye take cikin wata haɗaɗɗiyar shiga ta musamman HAJIYA LAYLERH keñan uwar kuma amarya a gidan ABDULJALAAL AREEFULLAHI MAI NASARA.
    Tun lokacin da queen ta soma zance itama ta kafe ta da nata kyawawan idanun, wani irin murmushi ta saki mai taushi da tsayawa aran duk wanda akawa tayi. Ahankali cikin isa, ƙasaita, ta shiga takowa parlourn, wanda har xuwa lokacin idanun mai girma Jalal nakan RAYUWAR SA LAYLERH, wani irin azababben kallo ya ke binta dashi.

    Sultan ne Yaɗan tura bakinsa gaba yana cewa.
   "Gaskiya wannan aikin sojan badai wahala ba papiey, nidai kawai kayi haƙuri na koma dactor kamar bro na'eem da ya ya Fateemerh kaji papiey!"
     
"Uhmm-uhmmm!"

Shine kawai abinda ya iya cewa domin zama da laylerh tayi saman sofar dake fuskantar sa kai tsaye, 

  Cike da tsananin qaunar junansu suka haɗe baki wajen faɗin.

"LA MIA VITAN PAPIEY barka da fitowaaaa" 
       Cike da tsananin qaunar yaran nata tace.
  "Yooooo guys barkanmu,  ku tsaya na rabaa muku rigimarku"

"Yeeeeeeeeee" matasan yaran mata da mazan suka shiga murna, inda tace su Sultan sukai afreen adda aknan, Sadeeq kuma daya kasance qarshen ƴan miskilancin duniya da mugun hali a kai shi gidan yaya lauwali domin shine kaɗai wanda ke take masa burki, Laylerh kuma tace tana wurin grennynta ummie. Haka dai duk kowa ta faɗi inda ya dace akai shi, shidai Sultan se cika yake hana bayarwa domin ba kaɗan ba ya tsani aikin sojan Nan, kuma sojan ma sojan sama, 

   Ahankali Queen ta miqe cikin siririyar muryanta tace.
     "Papiey naaa! Amiey  na tafi wurin ta qarfen, niyau cenñnn na nufa domin eena da exams, akwai karatu akaina na tabba su afreen barasu barni ba"

    Saida ta tsaya taja numfashi kana tace .
  "Good 9yt asennn xan kwana"

   "Allah ya tashe mu lafiya" 
   Iyayen suka haɗe wajen bata amsa ɗaya.
  Tana fita kuwa da wani irin sassarfa jalaal ya qaraso gaban Layla dake hakimce ta nutse cikin kujara, kansa ya daura saman cinyarta yana qara qusa kansa saman cinyar nata, da wata iriyar sarqaqaqiyar murya yayi amfani wajen faɗin.
    "Pls muje a kula dani la mia vita!" 
 Hannunta ta cusa saman lalluasar gashin dake nan luff tamkar saurayi ɗan shekaru ashirin.
      "Me ya sa sammm zumatyyyy baya ganin ya girma ne"
    Tayi Maganar da wata shu'umar murya.
   Cikin rawar da jikinsa ya soma nee jin da muryan da tayi amfani yace.

  "Kee Kee Kee ɗimce dai kene bakya taɓa sauya min my watermelon daima qara sugar da zuma kike, my Nan ♥️"
 

  Wani irin lumshe idanunta tayi jin yadda hannuwansa ke faman yawo saman sassan jikin ta, qara cusa hannunta tayi cikin tulin sumar kansa tana murzawa Ahankali.

    Gani suna a parlour ne yasan maleek ɗauqar abarsa cakkk yana yin bedroom nashi da ita, yana faɗin.
 "kamar fa akwai ajiya na Anan" yayi maganar yana shafa saman mararnta,  "uhmmmmm" 
   Shine kaɗai sautin data bada.
    Wani irin farin ciki ne ya kama Jalal jin ashe tana dauke da wani cikin masa tsayin shekaru har biyar, sai gashi Ubangijii ya kuma basu wani cikin lokacin da suka riga suka fawwalama Allah.
  A zatonsu ta gama haihuwarne, cikin deep saund ya raɗa mata.
   "Kinga keñan idan kika haihu sai muwa AMMEY LAYLERH kara marubuciyar littafin JALAAL AREEF da KAREN BANA mu sanya sunanta"

   Ɗan ware lumsassun idanunta tayi wanda suke cike da tsantsarn peelings na miji nata tace.
   "Kamar kasan abinda ke kwance a qasar raina keñan, yaci ace munwa AMMEY LAYLERHN kara kuwa wajen sanya sunanta, duk da itama sunanta MARYAM ƴar gaban goshin ƴarta kuma LAYLERH CE ✨"

  "Zanyi zamu mata kararn nan kuwa TABBAS tabbas TABBAS saina cika alqawarin Nan "

Haka dai rayuwar ta lula inda JALAAL AREEF ya cika alqawarinsa domin Daram ya sanya Sunan AMMEY LAYLERH wacce suke qira da MIRRAH 💫.

Alhamdulillah rayuwa tayi albarka  inda Sultan tuni an zama cikakken sojan sama yayinda da na'eem ya zama cikakken dactor.

Little Queen tayi aure a qasar data kasance ta mahaifinta a da wato ƙasar EGYPT.
